import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from sklearn.linear_model import LinearRegression
from sklearn.metrics import mean_squared_error
# ١ . (ﺧﺮوﺟﯽ و ورودی ھﺎی داده ) ﻧﻤﻮﻧﮫ داﻣﻨﮫ ﯾﮏ ﺳﺎﺧﺖ
np.random.seed(42)
P y t h o n

03.08.26, 22:23ﺗﻔﺎوت ﯾﺎدﮔﯾری ﻧظﺎرت ﺷده و ﻧظﺎرت ﻧﺷدهGoogle Gemini
https://gemini.google.com/app/18ccc48c4f028f50 14/101
ﺑردارﯾم؟ ﭼطور را ﺑﻌدی ﮔﺎم
X = np.linspace(1, 100, 50).reshape(-1, 1)
y = 2.5 * X.squeeze() + np.random.normal(0, 10, 50)
# ﻧﺨﺎﻟﮫ» ﭼﻨﺪ اﻓﺰودن» (Outlier) ﺑﺮﻧﺪ ﻣﯽ ﺑﺎﻻ را زﯾﺎن ﮐﮫ
y[5] += 150
y[42] -= 180
# ٢ . اﺳﺎس ﺑﺮ ﮔﯿﺮی ﻧﺨﺎﻟﮫZ-Score (دارﻧﺪ ﻓﺎﺻﻠﮫ ﻣﻌﯿﺎر اﻧﺤﺮاف ٢. ۵ از ﺑﯿﺶ ﮐﮫ ھﺎﯾﯽ داده ﺣﺬف) 
z_scores = np.abs((y - np.mean(y)) / np.std(y))
clean_mask = z_scores < 2.5
X_clean = X[clean_mask]
y_clean = y[clean_mask]
# ٣ . ﺷﺪه ﭘﺎﻻﯾﺶ ھﺎی داده روی( ﺷﺪه ﻧﻈﺎرت ﯾﺎدﮔﯿﺮی ) ﻧﮕﺎﺷﺖ ﺗﺎﺑﻊ ﺳﺎﺧﺖ
model = LinearRegression()
model.fit(X_clean, y_clean)
# ﭘﯿﻮﺳﺘﮫ داﻣﻨﮫ در ﺟﺪﯾﺪ ﻧﻘﺎط روی ﺑﯿﻨﯽ ﭘﯿﺶ
y_pred = model.predict(X_clean)
loss = mean_squared_error(y_clean, y_pred)
print(f"ﺷﺪه ﺣﺬف ھﺎی ﻧﺨﺎﻟﮫ ﺗﻌﺪاد : {np.sum(~clean_mask)}")
print(f"زﯾﺎن ﻣﯿﺰانLoss) ﮔﯿﺮی ﻧﺨﺎﻟﮫ از ﭘﺲ : {loss:.2f}")
print(f"(ﺧﻂ ﺷﯿﺐ ) ﻧﮕﺎﺷﺖ ﺗﺎﺑﻊ ﺿﺮﯾﺐ : {model.coef_[0]:.2f}")
:ﺷﻣﺎ رﯾﺎﺿﯾﺎت ﺑﺎ ارﺗﺑﺎط
ﺷﻮد؛ ﻣﻨﻈﻢ ھﺎ داده ﭘﻮش ﺗﺎ ﺷﺪﻧﺪ ﺣﺬف داﻣﻨﻪ از ھﺎ ﻧﺨﺎﻟﻪ اﺑﺘﺪا ﺑﺎﻻ، ﮐﺪ در

.ﮐﻨﺪ ﺑﯿﻨﯽ ﭘﯿﺶ ﭘﯿﻮﺳﺘﻪ ﻓﻀﺎی در را ھﺎ داده رﻓﺘﺎر ﺗﺎ دﯾﺪ آﻣﻮزش( F u n c t io n

ﭘﺎﯾﺗون در( ﻧﺷده ﻧظﺎرت روش) 

و ﮐﺎوی داده ﺑرای ﭘﺎﯾﺗون ﯾﺎدﮔﯾری راه ﻧﻘﺷﮫ
ﻣﺎﺷﯾن ﯾﺎدﮔﯾری

ﺣوزه در ﭘﺎﯾﺗون ﯾﺎدﮔﯾری ﺑرای ﮔﺎم ﺑﮫ ﮔﺎم( R o a d m a p ) راه ﻧﻘﺷﮫ
ﭼﯾﺳت؟ ﻣﺎﺷﯾن ﯾﺎدﮔﯾری و ﮐﺎوی داده
ﺑردارﯾم؟ ﭼطور را ﺑﻌدی ﮔﺎم
X = np.linspace(1, 100, 50).reshape(-1, 1)
y = 2.5 * X.squeeze() + np.random.normal(0, 10, 50)
# ﻧﺨﺎﻟﮫ» ﭼﻨﺪ اﻓﺰودن» (Outlier) ﺑﺮﻧﺪ ﻣﯽ ﺑﺎﻻ را زﯾﺎن ﮐﮫ
y[5] += 150
y[42] -= 180
# ٢ . اﺳﺎس ﺑﺮ ﮔﯿﺮی ﻧﺨﺎﻟﮫZ-Score (دارﻧﺪ ﻓﺎﺻﻠﮫ ﻣﻌﯿﺎر اﻧﺤﺮاف ٢. ۵ از ﺑﯿﺶ ﮐﮫ ھﺎﯾﯽ داده ﺣﺬف) 
z_scores = np.abs((y - np.mean(y)) / np.std(y))
clean_mask = z_scores < 2.5
X_clean = X[clean_mask]
y_clean = y[clean_mask]
# ٣ . ﺷﺪه ﭘﺎﻻﯾﺶ ھﺎی داده روی( ﺷﺪه ﻧﻈﺎرت ﯾﺎدﮔﯿﺮی ) ﻧﮕﺎﺷﺖ ﺗﺎﺑﻊ ﺳﺎﺧﺖ
model = LinearRegression()
model.fit(X_clean, y_clean)
# ﭘﯿﻮﺳﺘﮫ داﻣﻨﮫ در ﺟﺪﯾﺪ ﻧﻘﺎط روی ﺑﯿﻨﯽ ﭘﯿﺶ
y_pred = model.predict(X_clean)
loss = mean_squared_error(y_clean, y_pred)
print(f"ﺷﺪه ﺣﺬف ھﺎی ﻧﺨﺎﻟﮫ ﺗﻌﺪاد : {np.sum(~clean_mask)}")
print(f"زﯾﺎن ﻣﯿﺰانLoss) ﮔﯿﺮی ﻧﺨﺎﻟﮫ از ﭘﺲ : {loss:.2f}")
print(f"(ﺧﻂ ﺷﯿﺐ ) ﻧﮕﺎﺷﺖ ﺗﺎﺑﻊ ﺿﺮﯾﺐ : {model.coef_[0]:.2f}")
:ﺷﻣﺎ رﯾﺎﺿﯾﺎت ﺑﺎ ارﺗﺑﺎط

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
# ﻣﺎﺷﯿﻦ ﯾﺎدﮔﯿﺮی و ﮐﺎوی داده ھﺎی ﮐﺘﺎﺑﺨﺎﻧﮫ
from sklearn.model_selection import train_test_split
from sklearn.linear_model import LinearRegression
from sklearn.cluster import KMeans
from sklearn.metrics import mean_squared_error, silhouette_score

03.08.26, 22:23ﺗﻔﺎوت ﯾﺎدﮔﯾری ﻧظﺎرت ﺷده و ﻧظﺎرت ﻧﺷدهGoogle Gemini
https://gemini.google.com/app/18ccc48c4f028f50 18/101
# ================================================================
# ﺧﺎم ھﺎی داده ﺳﺎﺧﺖ و ﺑﺎرﮔﺬاری: ١ ﮔﺎمRaw Data Setup)
# ================================================================
np.random.seed(42)
# ﭘﯿﻮﺳﺘﮫ داﻣﻨﮫ ﯾﮏ در داده ٠٠۵ ﺳﺎﺧﺖ
X_raw = np.linspace(1, 100, 500).reshape(-1, 1)
y_raw = 3.5 * X_raw.squeeze() + np.random.normal(0, 15, 500)
# ﻧﺨﺎﻟﮫ» ٠١ ﻋﻤﺪی اﻓﺰودن» (Outlier) ﺑﺮﻧﺪ ﻣﯽ ﺑﺎﻻ ﺷﺪت ﺑﮫ را زﯾﺎن ﮐﮫ
outlier_indices = np.random.choice(500, 10, replace=False)
y_raw[outlier_indices] += np.random.choice([-200, 200], 10)
# ﺑﮫ ﺗﺒﺪﯾﻞDataFrame ھﺎ داده ﺗﺮ راﺣﺖ ﻣﺪﯾﺮﯾﺖ ﺑﺮای
df = pd.DataFrame({'Feature_X': X_raw.squeeze(), 'Target_Y': y_raw
print(f"ﺧﺎم ھﺎی داده ﮐﻞ ﺗﻌﺪاد : {len(df)}")
# ================================================================
# ھﺎ داده ﭘﺎﻻﯾﺶ و ﮔﯿﺮی ﻧﺨﺎﻟﮫ: ٢ ﮔﺎمData Cleaning / Noise Reduction)
# ================================================================
# ﻣﻌﯿﺎر از اﺳﺘﻔﺎدهZ-Score ھﺴﺘﻨﺪ اﺳﺘﺎﻧﺪارد داﻣﻨﮫ از ﺑﯿﺮون ﮐﮫ اﻋﻀﺎﯾﯽ ﺷﻨﺎﺳﺎﯾﯽ ﺑﺮای
mean_y = df['Target_Y'].mean()
std_y = df['Target_Y'].std()
# ﻣﯿﺎﻧﮕﯿﻦ از ﻣﻌﯿﺎر اﻧﺤﺮاف ٢. ۵ از ﺑﯿﺸﺘﺮ ﻓﺎﺻﻠﮫ: ﺑﻮدن ﻧﺨﺎﻟﮫ ﺷﺮط
df['Z_Score'] = (df['Target_Y'] - mean_y) / std_y
df_clean = df[np.abs(df['Z_Score']) < 2.5].copy()
outliers_count = len(df) - len(df_clean)
print(f"ﺷﺪه ﺣﺬف و ﺷﻨﺎﺳﺎﯾﯽ ھﺎی ﻧﺨﺎﻟﮫ ﺗﻌﺪاد : {outliers_count}")
print(f"ﺷﺪه ﺳﺎزی ﭘﺎک ھﺎی داده ﺗﻌﺪاد : {len(df_clean)}")
# ================================================================
# (زﯾﺎن ﮐﻤﺘﺮﯾﻦ ﺑﺎ ﻧﮕﺎﺷﺖ ﺗﺎﺑﻊ ﺳﺎﺧﺖ ) ﺷﺪه ﻧﻈﺎرت ﺳﺎزی ﻣﺪل: ٣ ﮔﺎم
================================================================ #
X = df_clean[['Feature_X']]
y = df_clean['Target_Y']
# آﻣﻮزش زﯾﺮﻣﺠﻤﻮﻋﮫ دو ﺑﮫ ھﺎ داده ﺗﻔﮑﯿﮏTrain) آزﻣﻮن وTest)
X_train, X_test, y_train, y_test = train_test_split(X, y, test_si
# ﺧﻄﯽ ﻧﮕﺎﺷﺖ ﺗﺎﺑﻊ آﻣﻮزش و ﺳﺎﺧﺖ
03.08.26, 22:23ﺗﻔﺎوت ﯾﺎدﮔﯾری ﻧظﺎرت ﺷده و ﻧظﺎرت ﻧﺷدهGoogle Gemini
https://gemini.google.com/app/18ccc48c4f028f50 19/101
model = LinearRegression()
model.fit(X_train, y_train)
# زﯾﺎن ﺗﺎﺑﻊ ﻣﺤﺎﺳﺒﮫ و ﺗﺴﺖ داﻣﻨﮫ روی ﺑﯿﻨﯽ ﭘﯿﺶMSE)
y_pred = model.predict(X_test)
loss_mse = mean_squared_error(y_test, y_pred)
print("\n--- ﺷﺪه ﻧﻈﺎرت ﻣﺪل ﻧﺘﺎﯾﺞ"--- )
print(f"(ﺷﯿﺐ ) ﻧﮕﺎﺷﺖ ﺗﺎﺑﻊ ﺿﺮﯾﺐ : {model.coef_[0]:.2f}")
print(f"ﻣﺒﺪأ از ﻋﺮض : {model.intercept_:.2f}")
print(f"ﺗﺴﺖ ھﺎی داده روی زﯾﺎن ﻣﯿﺰانMSE Loss): {loss_mse:.2f}")
# ================================================================
# (ھﺎ ﺧﻮﺷﮫ ﻣﺮاﮐﺰ ﯾﺎﻓﺘﻦ و ﻣﺠﻤﻮﻋﮫ اﻓﺮاز ) ﻧﺸﺪه ﻧﻈﺎرت ﺳﺎزی ﻣﺪل: ۴ ﮔﺎم
================================================================ #
 #ﺧﻮﺷﮫ ٣ ﺑﮫ ﺷﺪه ﺳﺎزی ﭘﺎک ھﺎی داده ﺑﻨﺪی دﺳﺘﮫCluster) ﺛﻘﻞ ﻣﺮاﮐﺰ ﺣﻮل
kmeans = KMeans(n_clusters=3, random_state=42, n_init=10)
df_clean['Cluster'] = kmeans.fit_predict(X)
# ﺳﯿﻠﻮﺋﺖ ﻣﻌﯿﺎر ﺑﺎ ﻓﻀﺎ اﻓﺮاز ﮐﯿﻔﯿﺖ ارزﯾﺎﺑﯽSilhouette Score)
score = silhouette_score(X, df_clean['Cluster'])
print("\n--- (ﺑﻨﺪی ﺧﻮﺷﮫ ) ﻧﺸﺪه ﻧﻈﺎرت ﻣﺪل ﻧﺘﺎﯾﺞ"--- )
print(f"ھﺎ ﺧﻮﺷﮫ ﻣﺮاﮐﺰCentroids):\n{kmeans.cluster_centers_.squeeze()
print(f"ﻓﻀﺎ اﻓﺮاز ﮐﯿﻔﯿﺖ ﺷﺎﺧﺺSilhouette Score): {score:.2f}")
# ================================================================
# ﻓﺮآوری ﻓﺮآﯾﻨﺪ ﻧﺘﺎﯾﺞ ﺳﺎزی ﺑﺼﺮی: ۵ ﮔﺎم
================================================================ #
plt.figure(figsize=(10, 5))
# ھﺎ ﻧﺨﺎﻟﮫ و ﭘﺎک ھﺎی داده رﺳﻢ
plt.scatter(df['Feature_X'], df['Target_Y'], color='red', alpha=0
plt.scatter(df_clean['Feature_X'], df_clean['Target_Y'], c=df_clea
# (رﮔﺮﺳﯿﻮن ﺧﻂ ) ﭘﯿﻮﺳﺘﮫ ﻧﮕﺎﺷﺖ ﺗﺎﺑﻊ رﺳﻢ
plt.plot(X, model.predict(X), color='black', linewidth=2, label='ﺖ
plt.title('ﺑﯿﻨﯽ ﭘﯿﺶ و ﺑﻨﺪی ﺧﻮﺷﮫ ﺳﺎزی، ﭘﺎک: ﮐﺎوی داده ﺗﻮﻟﯿﺪ ﺧﻂ' )
plt.xlabel('Feature X')
plt.ylabel('Target Y')
plt.legend()
model = LinearRegression()
model.fit(X_train, y_train)
# زﯾﺎن ﺗﺎﺑﻊ ﻣﺤﺎﺳﺒﮫ و ﺗﺴﺖ داﻣﻨﮫ روی ﺑﯿﻨﯽ ﭘﯿﺶMSE)
y_pred = model.predict(X_test)
loss_mse = mean_squared_error(y_test, y_pred)
print("\n--- ﺷﺪه ﻧﻈﺎرت ﻣﺪل ﻧﺘﺎﯾﺞ"--- )
print(f"(ﺷﯿﺐ ) ﻧﮕﺎﺷﺖ ﺗﺎﺑﻊ ﺿﺮﯾﺐ : {model.coef_[0]:.2f}")
print(f"ﻣﺒﺪأ از ﻋﺮض : {model.intercept_:.2f}")
print(f"ﺗﺴﺖ ھﺎی داده روی زﯾﺎن ﻣﯿﺰانMSE Loss): {loss_mse:.2f}")
# ================================================================
# (ھﺎ ﺧﻮﺷﮫ ﻣﺮاﮐﺰ ﯾﺎﻓﺘﻦ و ﻣﺠﻤﻮﻋﮫ اﻓﺮاز ) ﻧﺸﺪه ﻧﻈﺎرت ﺳﺎزی ﻣﺪل: ۴ ﮔﺎم
================================================================ #
 #ﺧﻮﺷﮫ ٣ ﺑﮫ ﺷﺪه ﺳﺎزی ﭘﺎک ھﺎی داده ﺑﻨﺪی دﺳﺘﮫCluster) ﺛﻘﻞ ﻣﺮاﮐﺰ ﺣﻮل
kmeans = KMeans(n_clusters=3, random_state=42, n_init=10)
df_clean['Cluster'] = kmeans.fit_predict(X)
# ﺳﯿﻠﻮﺋﺖ ﻣﻌﯿﺎر ﺑﺎ ﻓﻀﺎ اﻓﺮاز ﮐﯿﻔﯿﺖ ارزﯾﺎﺑﯽSilhouette Score)
score = silhouette_score(X, df_clean['Cluster'])
print("\n--- (ﺑﻨﺪی ﺧﻮﺷﮫ ) ﻧﺸﺪه ﻧﻈﺎرت ﻣﺪل ﻧﺘﺎﯾﺞ"--- )
print(f"ھﺎ ﺧﻮﺷﮫ ﻣﺮاﮐﺰCentroids):\n{kmeans.cluster_centers_.squeeze()
print(f"ﻓﻀﺎ اﻓﺮاز ﮐﯿﻔﯿﺖ ﺷﺎﺧﺺSilhouette Score): {score:.2f}")
# ================================================================
# ﻓﺮآوری ﻓﺮآﯾﻨﺪ ﻧﺘﺎﯾﺞ ﺳﺎزی ﺑﺼﺮی: ۵ ﮔﺎم
================================================================ #

plt.plot(X, model.predict(X), color='black', linewidth=2, label='ﺖ

plt.xlabel('Feature X')
plt.ylabel('Target Y')

03.08.26, 22:23ﺗﻔﺎوت ﯾﺎدﮔﯾری ﻧظﺎرت ﺷده و ﻧظﺎرت ﻧﺷدهGoogle Gemini

from sklearn.model_selection import train_test_split
from sklearn.linear_model import LinearRegression
from sklearn.cluster import KMeans
from sklearn.metrics import mean_squared_error, silhouette_score
# ================================================================
# ﻓﺎﯾﻞ ﺧﻮاﻧﺪن و درﯾﺎﻓﺖ: ١ ﮔﺎمCSV واﻗﻌﯽRead CSV File)
# ================================================================
file_path = 'data.csv' # ﻓﺎﯾﻞ آدرسCSV ﺷﻤﺎ
try:
 df_raw = pd.read_csv(file_path)
 print("ﺷﺪ ﺑﺎرﮔﺬاری ﻣﻮﻓﻘﯿﺖ ﺑﺎ ﻓﺎﯾﻞ". )
print(f"اوﻟﯿﮫ ھﺎی ﺳﺘﻮن و ھﺎ ردﯾﻒ ﮐﻞ ﺗﻌﺪاد : {df_raw.shape}")
except FileNotFoundError:
 print(f"ﻣﺴﯿﺮ در ﻓﺎﯾﻠﯽ: ﺧﻄﺎ' {file_path}' ﻧﺸﺪ ﭘﯿﺪا". )
exit()
# (ھﺪف ) ﺧﺮوﺟﯽ و( وﯾﮋﮔﯽ ) ورودی ھﺎی ﺳﺘﻮن ﮐﺮدن ﻣﺸﺨﺺ
 ** #ﻓﺎﯾﻞ ﺑﺎ ﻣﺘﻨﺎﺳﺐ را ھﺎ ﺳﺘﻮن ﻧﺎمCSV ﺑﺪه ﺗﻐﯿﯿﺮ ﺧﻮدت** 
feature_col = 'Feature_X' # ﻣﺜﻼًArea' ﯾﺎExperience'
target_col = 'Target_Y' # ﻣﺜﻼًPrice' ﯾﺎSalary'
# ﻣﻔﻘﻮد ﻣﻘﺪار ﺳﺘﻮن دو اﯾﻦ در ﮐﮫ ھﺎﯾﯽ ردﯾﻒ ﺣﺬفMissing Value) دارﻧﺪ
df = df_raw.dropna(subset=[feature_col, target_col]).copy()
# ================================================================
# ھﺪف ﻣﺘﻐﯿﺮ اﺳﺎس ﺑﺮ ﮔﯿﺮی ﻧﺨﺎﻟﮫ: ٢ ﮔﺎمData Cleaning)
# ================================================================
# ﻣﺤﺎﺳﺒﮫZ-Score ھﺪف ﺳﺘﻮن ﺑﺮای
mean_y = df[target_col].mean()
std_y = df[target_col].std()
df['Z_Score'] = (df[target_col] - mean_y) / std_y
# اﺳﺖ ﻣﻌﯿﺎر اﻧﺤﺮاف ٢. ۵ از ﮐﻤﺘﺮ ھﺎ آن ﻓﺎﺻﻠﮫ ﮐﮫ ھﺎﯾﯽ داده داﺷﺘﻦ ﻧﮕﮫ
df_clean = df[np.abs(df['Z_Score']) < 2.5].copy()
outliers_count = len(df) - len(df_clean)
print(f"ﺷﺪه ﺣﺬف ھﺎی ﻧﺨﺎﻟﮫ ﺗﻌﺪاد : {outliers_count}")
print(f"ﺷﺪه ﺳﺎزی ﭘﺎک ھﺎی داده ﺗﻌﺪاد : {len(df_clean)}")
# ================================================================
03.08.26, 22:23ﺗﻔﺎوت ﯾﺎدﮔﯾری ﻧظﺎرت ﺷده و ﻧظﺎرت ﻧﺷدهGoogle Gemini
https://gemini.google.com/app/18ccc48c4f028f50 22/101
# (واﻗﻌﯽ ھﺎی داده روی ﻧﮕﺎﺷﺖ ﺗﺎﺑﻊ ﺳﺎﺧﺖ ) ﺷﺪه ﻧﻈﺎرت ﺳﺎزی ﻣﺪل: ٣ ﮔﺎم
================================================================ #
X = df_clean[[feature_col]]
y = df_clean[target_col]
# آزﻣﻮن و آﻣﻮزش ﺑﺨﺶ دو ﺑﮫ ﺷﺪه ﭘﺎک ھﺎی داده ﺗﻘﺴﯿﻢ
X_train, X_test, y_train, y_test = train_test_split(X, y, test_si
# ﻧﮕﺎﺷﺖ ﺗﺎﺑﻊ آﻣﻮزش
model = LinearRegression()
model.fit(X_train, y_train)
# زﯾﺎن ﻣﯿﺰان ارزﯾﺎﺑﯽMSE) ﺗﺴﺖ ھﺎی داده روی
y_pred = model.predict(X_test)
loss_mse = mean_squared_error(y_test, y_pred)
print("\n--- ﺷﺪه ﻧﻈﺎرت ﻣﺪل ﻧﺘﺎﯾﺞ"--- )
print(f"(ﺷﯿﺐ ) ﺗﺎﺑﻊ ﺿﺮﯾﺐ : {model.coef_[0]:.4f}")
print(f"ﻣﺒﺪأ از ﻋﺮض : {model.intercept_:.4f}")
print(f"زﯾﺎن ﻣﯿﺰانMSE Loss): {loss_mse:.4f}")
# ================================================================
# (واﻗﻌﯽ ھﺎی داده ﺑﻨﺪی ﺧﻮﺷﮫ و اﻓﺮاز ) ﻧﺸﺪه ﻧﻈﺎرت ﺳﺎزی ﻣﺪل: ۴ ﮔﺎم
================================================================ #
 #ﻣﺮاﮐﺰ ﺣﻮل ﺧﻮﺷﮫ ٣ ﺑﮫ ھﺎ داده اﻓﺮاز
kmeans = KMeans(n_clusters=3, random_state=42, n_init=10)
df_clean['Cluster'] = kmeans.fit_predict(X)
score = silhouette_score(X, df_clean['Cluster'])
print("\n--- ﻧﺸﺪه ﻧﻈﺎرت ﻣﺪل ﻧﺘﺎﯾﺞ"--- )
print(f"ھﺎ ﺧﻮﺷﮫ ﻣﺮاﮐﺰCentroids):\n{kmeans.cluster_centers_.squeeze()
print(f"ﺑﻨﺪی ﺧﻮﺷﮫ ﮐﯿﻔﯿﺖ ﺷﺎﺧﺺSilhouette Score): {score:.4f}")
# ================================================================
# ﻓﺎﯾﻞ ﯾﮏ در ﺷﺪه ﭘﺎﻻﯾﺶ ﺧﺮوﺟﯽ ذﺧﯿﺮه: ۵ ﮔﺎمCSV ﺟﺪﯾﺪ
================================================================ #
 #ﮐﻤﮑﯽ ﺳﺘﻮن ﺣﺬفZ_Score ذﺧﯿﺮه از ﻗﺒﻞ
df_clean_to_save = df_clean.drop(columns=['Z_Score'])
output_file_path = 'cleaned_data_with_clusters.csv'
# (واﻗﻌﯽ ھﺎی داده روی ﻧﮕﺎﺷﺖ ﺗﺎﺑﻊ ﺳﺎﺧﺖ ) ﺷﺪه ﻧﻈﺎرت ﺳﺎزی ﻣﺪل: ٣ ﮔﺎم
================================================================ #

# آزﻣﻮن و آﻣﻮزش ﺑﺨﺶ دو ﺑﮫ ﺷﺪه ﭘﺎک ھﺎی داده ﺗﻘﺴﯿﻢ

print(f"ﻣﺒﺪأ از ﻋﺮض : {model.intercept_:.4f}")
print(f"زﯾﺎن ﻣﯿﺰانMSE Loss): {loss_mse:.4f}")
# ================================================================
# (واﻗﻌﯽ ھﺎی داده ﺑﻨﺪی ﺧﻮﺷﮫ و اﻓﺮاز ) ﻧﺸﺪه ﻧﻈﺎرت ﺳﺎزی ﻣﺪل: ۴ ﮔﺎم
================================================================ #
 #ﻣﺮاﮐﺰ ﺣﻮل ﺧﻮﺷﮫ ٣ ﺑﮫ ھﺎ داده اﻓﺮاز
kmeans = KMeans(n_clusters=3, random_state=42, n_init=10)

print(f"ھﺎ ﺧﻮﺷﮫ ﻣﺮاﮐﺰCentroids):\n{kmeans.cluster_centers_.squeeze()
print(f"ﺑﻨﺪی ﺧﻮﺷﮫ ﮐﯿﻔﯿﺖ ﺷﺎﺧﺺSilhouette Score): {score:.4f}")
# ================================================================
# ﻓﺎﯾﻞ ﯾﮏ در ﺷﺪه ﭘﺎﻻﯾﺶ ﺧﺮوﺟﯽ ذﺧﯿﺮه: ۵ ﮔﺎمCSV ﺟﺪﯾﺪ

import numpy as np
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
from sklearn.linear_model import lasso_path
from sklearn.datasets import make_regression
# ١ . وﯾﮋﮔﯽ ٠٢ ﺑﺎ ﻧﻤﻮﻧﮫ داده ﺳﺎﺧﺖ
X, y = make_regression(n_samples=100, n_features=20, n_informative
# ٢ . ﻻﻧﺪاھﺎ از ای داﻣﻨﮫ ﺑﺮای ﺿﺮاﯾﺐ ﻣﺴﯿﺮ ﻣﺤﺎﺳﺒﮫ
alphas_lasso, coefs_lasso, _ = lasso_path(X, y)
# ٣ . ﻣﺴﯿﺮ ﻧﻤﻮدار رﺳﻢ
plt.figure(figsize=(10, 6))
for i in range(coefs_lasso.shape[0]):
 plt.plot(np.log10(alphas_lasso), coefs_lasso[i], label=f'Feat
plt.xlabel('log10(Lambda / Alpha)')
plt.ylabel('ھﺎ وﯾﮋﮔﯽ ﺿﺮاﯾﺐCoefficients)')
plt.title('رﮔﺮﺳﯿﻮن در ﺿﺮاﯾﺐ ﻣﺴﯿﺮ ﻧﻤﻮدارLasso (Lasso Path Plot)')
plt.grid(True)
plt.axhline(0, color='black', linestyle='--', linewidth=0.8)
plt.gca().invert_xaxis() # ﺑﺰرگ ﺑﮫ ﮐﻮﭼﮏ ﻻﻧﺪای از ﺣﺮﮐﺖ ﺑﺮای اﻓﻘﯽ ﻣﺤﻮر ﮐﺮدن س

import numpy as np
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
from sklearn.linear_model import lasso_path
from sklearn.datasets import make_regression
# ١ . وﯾﮋﮔﯽ ٠٢ ﺑﺎ ﻧﻤﻮﻧﮫ داده ﺳﺎﺧﺖ
X, y = make_regression(n_samples=100, n_features=20, n_informa
# ٢ . ﻻﻧﺪاھﺎ از ای داﻣﻨﮫ ﺑﺮای ﺿﺮاﯾﺐ ﻣﺴﯿﺮ ﻣﺤﺎﺳﺒﮫ
alphas_lasso, coefs_lasso, _ = lasso_path(X, y)
# ٣ . ﺿﺮاﯾﺐ ﻣﺴﯿﺮ ﻧﻤﻮدار رﺳﻢ
plt.figure(figsize=(10, 6))
for i in range(coefs_lasso.shape[0]):
 plt.plot(np.log10(alphas_lasso), coefs_lasso[i], label=f'F
plt.xlabel('log10(Lambda / Alpha)')
plt.ylabel('Coefficients')
plt.title('Lasso Path Plot')
plt.grid(True)
plt.axhline(0, color='black', linestyle='--', linewidth=0.8)

import numpy as np
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
from sklearn.linear_model import lasso_path
from sklearn.datasets import make_regression
# ١ . وﯾﮋﮔﯽ ٠٢ ﺑﺎ ﻧﻤﻮﻧﮫ داده ﺳﺎﺧﺖ
X, y = make_regression(n_samples=100, n_features=20, n_informa
# ٢ . ﻻﻧﺪاھﺎ از ای داﻣﻨﮫ ﺑﺮای ﺿﺮاﯾﺐ ﻣﺴﯿﺮ ﻣﺤﺎﺳﺒﮫ
alphas_lasso, coefs_lasso, _ = lasso_path(X, y)
# ٣ . ﺿﺮاﯾﺐ ﻣﺴﯿﺮ ﻧﻤﻮدار رﺳﻢ
plt.figure(figsize=(10, 6))
for i in range(coefs_lasso.shape[0]):
 plt.plot(np.log10(alphas_lasso), coefs_lasso[i], label=f'F
plt.xlabel('log10(Lambda / Alpha)')
plt.ylabel('Coefficients')
plt.title('Lasso Path Plot')
plt.grid(True)
plt.axhline(0, color='black', linestyle='--', linewidth=0.8)

import numpy as np
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
from sklearn.linear_model import lasso_path
from sklearn.datasets import make_regression
# ١ . وﯾﮋﮔﯽ ٠٢ ﺑﺎ ﻧﻤﻮﻧﮫ داده ﺳﺎﺧﺖ
X, y = make_regression(n_samples=100, n_features=20, n_informative
# ٢ . ﻻﻧﺪاھﺎ از ای داﻣﻨﮫ ﺑﺮای ﺿﺮاﯾﺐ ﻣﺴﯿﺮ ﻣﺤﺎﺳﺒﮫ
alphas_lasso, coefs_lasso, _ = lasso_path(X, y)
# ٣ . ﺿﺮاﯾﺐ ﻣﺴﯿﺮ ﻧﻤﻮدار رﺳﻢ
plt.figure(figsize=(10, 6))
for i in range(coefs_lasso.shape[0]):
 plt.plot(np.log10(alphas_lasso), coefs_lasso[i], label=f'Feat
plt.xlabel('log10(Lambda / Alpha)')
plt.ylabel('Coefficients')
plt.title('Lasso Path Plot')
plt.grid(True)
plt.axhline(0, color='black', linestyle='--', linewidth=0.8)
plt.gca().invert_xaxis()
# ﺟﺎی ﺑﮫplt.show()، ﮐﻨﯿﻢ ﻣﯽ ذﺧﯿﺮه ﭘﺮوژه ﻓﻮﻟﺪر در را ﻧﻤﻮدار ﻋﮑﺲ: 
plt.savefig('lasso_plot.png', dpi=300)
print("ﻋﮑﺲ ﻋﻨﻮان ﺑﮫ ﻣﻮﻓﻘﯿﺖ ﺑﺎ ﻧﻤﻮدارlasso_plot.png ﺷﺪ ذﺧﯿﺮه ﺷﻤﺎ ﻓﻮﻟﺪر در"! )

import numpy as np
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
from sklearn.linear_model import lasso_path
from sklearn.datasets import make_regression
# ١ . وﯾﮋﮔﯽ ٠٢ ﺑﺎ ﻧﻤﻮﻧﮫ داده ﺳﺎﺧﺖ
X, y = make_regression(n_samples=100, n_features=20, n_informative
# ٢ . ﻻﻧﺪاھﺎ از ای داﻣﻨﮫ ﺑﺮای ﺿﺮاﯾﺐ ﻣﺴﯿﺮ ﻣﺤﺎﺳﺒﮫ
alphas_lasso, coefs_lasso, _ = lasso_path(X, y)
# ٣ . ﺿﺮاﯾﺐ ﻣﺴﯿﺮ ﻧﻤﻮدار رﺳﻢ
plt.figure(figsize=(10, 6))
for i in range(coefs_lasso.shape[0]):
 plt.plot(np.log10(alphas_lasso), coefs_lasso[i], label=f'Feat
plt.xlabel('log10(Lambda / Alpha)')
plt.ylabel('Coefficients')
plt.title('Lasso Path Plot')
plt.grid(True)
plt.axhline(0, color='black', linestyle='--', linewidth=0.8)
plt.gca().invert_xaxis()
# ﺟﺎی ﺑﮫplt.show()، ﮐﻨﯿﻢ ﻣﯽ ذﺧﯿﺮه ﭘﺮوژه ﻓﻮﻟﺪر در را ﻧﻤﻮدار ﻋﮑﺲ: 
plt.savefig('lasso_plot.png', dpi=300)
print("ﻋﮑﺲ ﻋﻨﻮان ﺑﮫ ﻣﻮﻓﻘﯿﺖ ﺑﺎ ﻧﻤﻮدارlasso_plot.png ﺷﺪ ذﺧﯿﺮه ﺷﻤﺎ ﻓﻮﻟﺪر در"! )

import matplotlib
matplotlib.use('Agg') # ﮔﺮاﻓﯿﮑﯽ ﺧﻄﺎی از ﺟﻠﻮﮔﯿﺮیTcl
import numpy as np
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
from sklearn.linear_model import lasso_path
from sklearn.datasets import make_regression
# ١ . وﯾﮋﮔﯽ ٠٢ ﺑﺎ ﻧﻤﻮﻧﮫ داده ﺳﺎﺧﺖ
X, y = make_regression(n_samples=100, n_features=20, n_informative
# ٢ . ﻻﻧﺪاھﺎ از ای داﻣﻨﮫ ﺑﺮای ﺿﺮاﯾﺐ ﻣﺴﯿﺮ ﻣﺤﺎﺳﺒﮫ
alphas_lasso, coefs_lasso, _ = lasso_path(X, y)
# ٣ . ﺿﺮاﯾﺐ ﻣﺴﯿﺮ ﻧﻤﻮدار رﺳﻢ
plt.figure(figsize=(12, 6)) # ﺑﺎﺷﺪ ﺟﺎ راھﻨﻤﺎ ﺑﺮای ﺗﺎ ﮐﺮدﯾﻢ ﺑﯿﺸﺘﺮ ﮐﻤﯽ را ﺷﮑﻞ ﺮض
for i in range(coefs_lasso.shape[0]):
# رﻧﮕﯽ ﺧﻂ ھﺮ ﮔﺬاری ﻧﺎمLabel) در اﺳﺘﻔﺎده ﺑﺮایLegend
 plt.plot(np.log10(alphas_lasso), coefs_lasso[i], label=f'Feat
plt.xlabel('log10(Lambda / Alpha)')
plt.ylabel('Coefficients')
plt.title('Lasso Path Plot with Legend')
plt.grid(True)
plt.axhline(0, color='black', linestyle='--', linewidth=0.8)
plt.gca().invert_xaxis()
# ================================================================
# راھﻨﻤﺎ ﮐﺮدن اﺿﺎﻓﮫLegend) ﻧﻤﻮدار ﮐﺎدر از ﺧﺎرج در ﻣﺮﺗﺐ ﺻﻮرت ﺑﮫ
================================================================ #
plt.legend(
 bbox_to_anchor=(1.02, 1), # ﻧﻤﻮدار راﺳﺖ ﺳﻤﺖ ﺑﯿﺮون ﺑﮫ راھﻨﻤﺎ اﻧﺘﻘﺎل
loc='upper left', # ﺑﺎﻻ ﻧﻘﻄﮫ ﺑﮫ راھﻨﻤﺎ ﭼﭗِ ﺳﻤﺖ ﮔﻮﺷﮫ ﭼﺴﺒﺎﻧﺪن
borderaxespad=0, # ﮐﺎدر از ﺻﻔﺮ ﻓﺎﺻﻠﮫ
ncols=2, # ﻣﺠﺰا ﺳﺘﻮن ٢ در ھﺎ وﯾﮋﮔﯽ ﭼﯿﺪﻣﺎن
fontsize='small' # زﯾﺒﺎﯾﯽ ﺑﺮای ﺗﺮ ﮐﻮﭼﮏ ﻓﻮﻧﺖ اﻧﺪازه
)
 #راھﻨﻤﺎ ﮐﺎﻣﻞ ﮐﺎدر ﺣﻔﻆ ﺑﺎ ﻋﮑﺲ ذﺧﯿﺮه

import matplotlib
matplotlib.use('Agg') # ﮔﺮاﻓﯿﮑﯽ ﺧﻄﺎی از ﺟﻠﻮﮔﯿﺮیTcl
import numpy as np
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
from sklearn.linear_model import lasso_path
from sklearn.datasets import make_regression
# ١ . وﯾﮋﮔﯽ ٠٢ ﺑﺎ ﻧﻤﻮﻧﮫ داده ﺳﺎﺧﺖ
X, y = make_regression(n_samples=100, n_features=20, n_informative
# ٢ . ﻻﻧﺪاھﺎ از ای داﻣﻨﮫ ﺑﺮای ﺿﺮاﯾﺐ ﻣﺴﯿﺮ ﻣﺤﺎﺳﺒﮫ
alphas_lasso, coefs_lasso, _ = lasso_path(X, y)
# ٣ . ﺿﺮاﯾﺐ ﻣﺴﯿﺮ ﻧﻤﻮدار رﺳﻢ
plt.figure(figsize=(12, 6)) # ﺑﺎﺷﺪ ﺟﺎ راھﻨﻤﺎ ﺑﺮای ﺗﺎ ﮐﺮدﯾﻢ ﺑﯿﺸﺘﺮ ﮐﻤﯽ را ﺷﮑﻞ ﺮض
for i in range(coefs_lasso.shape[0]):
# رﻧﮕﯽ ﺧﻂ ھﺮ ﮔﺬاری ﻧﺎمLabel) در اﺳﺘﻔﺎده ﺑﺮایLegend
 plt.plot(np.log10(alphas_lasso), coefs_lasso[i], label=f'Feat
plt.xlabel('log10(Lambda / Alpha)')
plt.ylabel('Coefficients')
plt.title('Lasso Path Plot with Legend')
plt.grid(True)
plt.axhline(0, color='black', linestyle='--', linewidth=0.8)
plt.gca().invert_xaxis()
# ================================================================
# راھﻨﻤﺎ ﮐﺮدن اﺿﺎﻓﮫLegend) ﻧﻤﻮدار ﮐﺎدر از ﺧﺎرج در ﻣﺮﺗﺐ ﺻﻮرت ﺑﮫ
================================================================ #
plt.legend(
 bbox_to_anchor=(1.02, 1), # ﻧﻤﻮدار راﺳﺖ ﺳﻤﺖ ﺑﯿﺮون ﺑﮫ راھﻨﻤﺎ اﻧﺘﻘﺎل
loc='upper left', # ﺑﺎﻻ ﻧﻘﻄﮫ ﺑﮫ راھﻨﻤﺎ ﭼﭗِ ﺳﻤﺖ ﮔﻮﺷﮫ ﭼﺴﺒﺎﻧﺪن
borderaxespad=0, # ﮐﺎدر از ﺻﻔﺮ ﻓﺎﺻﻠﮫ
ncols=2, # ﻣﺠﺰا ﺳﺘﻮن ٢ در ھﺎ وﯾﮋﮔﯽ ﭼﯿﺪﻣﺎن
fontsize='small' # زﯾﺒﺎﯾﯽ ﺑﺮای ﺗﺮ ﮐﻮﭼﮏ ﻓﻮﻧﺖ اﻧﺪازه
)
 #راھﻨﻤﺎ ﮐﺎﻣﻞ ﮐﺎدر ﺣﻔﻆ ﺑﺎ ﻋﮑﺲ ذﺧﯿﺮه

import matplotlib
matplotlib.use('Agg') # ﮔﺮاﻓﯿﮑﯽ ﺧﻄﺎی از ﺟﻠﻮﮔﯿﺮیTcl
import numpy as np
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
from sklearn.linear_model import lasso_path
from sklearn.datasets import make_regression
# ١ . وﯾﮋﮔﯽ ٠٢ ﺑﺎ ﻧﻤﻮﻧﮫ داده ﺳﺎﺧﺖ
X, y = make_regression(n_samples=100, n_features=20, n_informative
# ٢ . ﻻﻧﺪاھﺎ از ای داﻣﻨﮫ ﺑﺮای ﺿﺮاﯾﺐ ﻣﺴﯿﺮ ﻣﺤﺎﺳﺒﮫ
alphas_lasso, coefs_lasso, _ = lasso_path(X, y)
# ٣ . ﻧﻤﻮدار رﺳﻢ
plt.figure(figsize=(12, 7))
log_alphas = np.log10(alphas_lasso)
for i in range(coefs_lasso.shape[0]):
# وﯾﮋﮔﯽ ھﺮ ﺧﻂ رﺳﻢ
line = plt.plot(log_alphas, coefs_lasso[i], alpha=0.8, linewid
 line_color = line[0].get_color()
# (دارد ﻗﺮار ﺗﺮ ﮐﻮﭼﮏ ﻻﻧﺪای ﮐﮫ ﺟﺎﯾﯽ ) ﭼﭗ ﺳﻤﺖ در ﺧﻂ ﺷﺮوع ﻧﻘﻄﮫ ﻣﺨﺘﺼﺎت ﮔﺮﻓﺘﻦ
x_start = log_alphas[0]
 y_start = coefs_lasso[i][0]
# ﻧﻮﯾﺴﯿﻢ ﻣﯽ ﻧﻤﻮدار روی ﻣﺴﺘﻘﯿﻢ را ﺿﺮﯾﺐ اوﻟﯿﮫ ﻣﻘﺪار و وﯾﮋﮔﯽ ﻧﺎم ﻧﺒﺎﺷﺪ، ﺻﻔﺮ ﺑﮫ ﻧﺰدﯾﮏ ﺧﯿﻠﯽ 
if abs(y_start) > 0.5:
 plt.text(
 x_start, y_start, 
f' F{i+1} ({y_start:.1f})', # ﺿﺮﯾﺐ اوﻟﯿﮫ ﻣﻘﺪار + وﯾﮋﮔﯽ ﻧﺎم 
color=line_color,
 fontsize=8,
 verticalalignment='center',
 fontweight='bold'
 )
plt.xlabel('log10(Lambda / Alpha)')
plt.ylabel('Coefficients Value')
plt.title('Lasso Path Plot with Direct Feature Labels & Coefficie
plt.grid(True, linestyle='--', alpha=0.5)
plt.axhline(0, color='black', linestyle='-', linewidth=0.8)

import numpy as np
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
from sklearn.linear_model import lasso_path
from sklearn.datasets import make_regression
# ١ . وﯾﮋﮔﯽ ٠٢ ﺑﺎ ﻧﻤﻮﻧﮫ داده ﺳﺎﺧﺖ
X, y = make_regression(n_samples=100, n_features=20, n_informative
# ٢ . ﻻﻧﺪاھﺎ از ای داﻣﻨﮫ ﺑﺮای ﺿﺮاﯾﺐ ﻣﺴﯿﺮ ﻣﺤﺎﺳﺒﮫ
alphas_lasso, coefs_lasso, _ = lasso_path(X, y)
# ٣ . رﺳﻢ ﺻﻔﺤﮫ ﺗﻨﻈﯿﻢ
plt.figure(figsize=(12, 7))
log_alphas = np.log10(alphas_lasso)
# ۴ . ﺧﻂ ھﺮ روی ﻣﺴﺘﻘﯿﻢ ﻣﺘﻨﯽ ﺑﺮﭼﺴﺐ ھﻤﺮاه ﺑﮫ ﺧﻄﻮط رﺳﻢ
for i in range(coefs_lasso.shape[0]):
# وﯾﮋﮔﯽ ﺧﻂ رﺳﻢ
lines = plt.plot(log_alphas, coefs_lasso[i], linewidth=2, alp
 line_color = lines[0].get_color()
# (دارد ﻗﺮار ﺗﺮ ﮐﻮﭼﮏ ﻻﻧﺪای ﮐﮫ ﺟﺎﯾﯽ ) ﺧﻂ ﭼﭗ ﺳﻤﺖ اﻧﺘﮭﺎی ﻧﻘﻄﮫ
x_pos = log_alphas[-1] 
 y_pos = coefs_lasso[i][-1]
# ﮐﻦ ﭼﺎپ ﻣﺘﻦ دارﻧﺪ ﻏﯿﺮﺻﻔﺮ ﺿﺮﯾﺐ ﮐﮫ ﻣﻮﺛﺮ ھﺎی وﯾﮋﮔﯽ ﺑﺮای ﻓﻘﻂ
if abs(y_pos) > 1.0:
 plt.text(
 x_pos, y_pos, 
f' F{i+1} (w={y_pos:.1f})', # ﺿﺮﯾﺐ ﻋﺪدی ﻣﻘﺪار + وﯾﮋﮔﯽ ﻧﺎم
color=line_color,
 fontsize=9,
 fontweight='bold',
 verticalalignment='center'
 )
# ۵ . ﻋﻨﻮان و ﻣﺤﻮرھﺎ ﺗﻨﻈﯿﻤﺎت
plt.xlabel('log10(Lambda / Alpha)', fontsize=11)
plt.ylabel('Coefficients Value (w)', fontsize=11)
plt.title('Lasso Path Plot with Direct Feature Labels & Coefficie
plt.grid(True, linestyle='--', alpha=0.6)
plt.axhline(0, color='black', linestyle='-', linewidth=1)
import numpy as np
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
from sklearn.linear_model import lasso_path
from sklearn.datasets import make_regression
# ١ . وﯾﮋﮔﯽ ٠٢ ﺑﺎ ﻧﻤﻮﻧﮫ داده ﺳﺎﺧﺖ
X, y = make_regression(n_samples=100, n_features=20, n_informative
# ٢ . ﻻﻧﺪاھﺎ از ای داﻣﻨﮫ ﺑﺮای ﺿﺮاﯾﺐ ﻣﺴﯿﺮ ﻣﺤﺎﺳﺒﮫ
alphas_lasso, coefs_lasso, _ = lasso_path(X, y)

# وﯾﮋﮔﯽ ﺧﻂ رﺳﻢ

x_pos = log_alphas[-1] 
 y_pos = coefs_lasso[i][-1]
# ﮐﻦ ﭼﺎپ ﻣﺘﻦ دارﻧﺪ ﻏﯿﺮﺻﻔﺮ ﺿﺮﯾﺐ ﮐﮫ ﻣﻮﺛﺮ ھﺎی وﯾﮋﮔﯽ ﺑﺮای ﻓﻘﻂ
if abs(y_pos) > 1.0:
 plt.text(
 x_pos, y_pos, 
f' F{i+1} (w={y_pos:.1f})', # ﺿﺮﯾﺐ ﻋﺪدی ﻣﻘﺪار + وﯾﮋﮔﯽ ﻧﺎم
color=line_color,
 fontsize=9,
 fontweight='bold',
 verticalalignment='center'
 )
# ۵ . ﻋﻨﻮان و ﻣﺤﻮرھﺎ ﺗﻨﻈﯿﻤﺎت
plt.xlabel('log10(Lambda / Alpha)', fontsize=11)
plt.ylabel('Coefficients Value (w)', fontsize=11)
plt.title('Lasso Path Plot with Direct Feature Labels & Coefficie
plt.grid(True, linestyle='--', alpha=0.6)

import numpy as np
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
from sklearn.linear_model import lasso_path
from sklearn.datasets import make_regression
# ١ . ﻧﻤﻮﻧﮫ داده ﺳﺎﺧﺖ
X, y = make_regression(n_samples=100, n_features=20, n_informative
# ٢ . ﺿﺮاﯾﺐ ﻣﺴﯿﺮ ﻣﺤﺎﺳﺒﮫ
alphas_lasso, coefs_lasso, _ = lasso_path(X, y)
# ٣ . (راھﻨﻤﺎ ﺟﺎﮔﯿﺮی ﺑﺮای ﺗﺮ ﻋﺮﯾﺾ ﮐﻤﯽ ) ﻧﻤﻮدار ﮐﺎدر ﺗﻨﻈﯿﻢ
plt.figure(figsize=(12, 6))
# ۴ . ﺑﺮای دﻗﯿﻖ ﻟﯿﺒﻞ ﺑﺎ ھﻤﺮاه ﺧﻂ ھﺮ رﺳﻢLegend
for i in range(coefs_lasso.shape[0]):
# (ﺿﺮﯾﺐ ﺑﺎﻻﺗﺮﯾﻦ = ﻻﻧﺪا ﮐﻤﺘﺮﯾﻦ ) ﻧﻤﻮدار راﺳﺖ ﺳﻤﺖ در ﺿﺮﯾﺐ اوﻟﯿﮫ ﻣﻘﺪار
initial_coef = coefs_lasso[i][-1]
# ﺿﺮﯾﺐ ﻣﻘﺪار و وﯾﮋﮔﯽ ﺷﻤﺎره ﺷﺎﻣﻞ ﻟﯿﺒﻞ
label_text = f'Feature {i+1} (w = {initial_coef:.1f})'
 plt.plot(np.log10(alphas_lasso), coefs_lasso[i], label=label_t
# ۵ . ﻋﻨﻮان و ﻣﺤﻮرھﺎ ﺗﻨﻈﯿﻤﺎت
plt.xlabel('log10(Lambda / Alpha)', fontsize=11)
plt.ylabel('Coefficients', fontsize=11)
plt.title('Lasso Path Plot with Coefficients Legend', fontsize=13
plt.grid(True)
plt.axhline(0, color='black', linestyle='--', linewidth=0.8)
plt.gca().invert_xaxis()
# ================================================================
# ۶ . ﮐﺮدن اﺿﺎﻓﮫLegend راﺳﺖ ﮐﺎدر از ﺧﺎرج در ﻣﺮﺗﺐ و ﮐﺎﻣﻞ

import numpy as np
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
from sklearn.linear_model import lasso_path
from sklearn.datasets import make_regression
# ١ . ﻧﻤﻮﻧﮫ داده ﺳﺎﺧﺖ
X, y = make_regression(n_samples=100, n_features=20, n_informative
# ٢ . ﺿﺮاﯾﺐ ﻣﺴﯿﺮ ﻣﺤﺎﺳﺒﮫ
alphas_lasso, coefs_lasso, _ = lasso_path(X, y)
# ٣ . (راھﻨﻤﺎ ﺟﺎﮔﯿﺮی ﺑﺮای ﺗﺮ ﻋﺮﯾﺾ ﮐﻤﯽ ) ﻧﻤﻮدار ﮐﺎدر ﺗﻨﻈﯿﻢ
plt.figure(figsize=(12, 6))
# ۴ . ﺑﺮای دﻗﯿﻖ ﻟﯿﺒﻞ ﺑﺎ ھﻤﺮاه ﺧﻂ ھﺮ رﺳﻢLegend
for i in range(coefs_lasso.shape[0]):
# (ﺿﺮﯾﺐ ﺑﺎﻻﺗﺮﯾﻦ = ﻻﻧﺪا ﮐﻤﺘﺮﯾﻦ ) ﻧﻤﻮدار راﺳﺖ ﺳﻤﺖ در ﺿﺮﯾﺐ اوﻟﯿﮫ ﻣﻘﺪار
initial_coef = coefs_lasso[i][-1]
# ﺿﺮﯾﺐ ﻣﻘﺪار و وﯾﮋﮔﯽ ﺷﻤﺎره ﺷﺎﻣﻞ ﻟﯿﺒﻞ
label_text = f'Feature {i+1} (w = {initial_coef:.1f})'
 plt.plot(np.log10(alphas_lasso), coefs_lasso[i], label=label_t
# ۵ . ﻋﻨﻮان و ﻣﺤﻮرھﺎ ﺗﻨﻈﯿﻤﺎت
plt.xlabel('log10(Lambda / Alpha)', fontsize=11)
plt.ylabel('Coefficients', fontsize=11)
plt.title('Lasso Path Plot with Coefficients Legend', fontsize=13
plt.grid(True)
plt.axhline(0, color='black', linestyle='--', linewidth=0.8)
plt.gca().invert_xaxis()

import numpy as np
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
from sklearn.linear_model import lasso_path
from sklearn.datasets import make_regression
import mplcursors # اﯾﺠﺎد ﮐﺘﺎﺑﺨﺎﻧﮫTooltip ﺗﻌﺎﻣﻠﯽ ﮐﻠﯿﮏ و
 #١ .ﻧﻤﻮﻧﮫ داده ﺳﺎﺧﺖ
X, y = make_regression(n_samples=100, n_features=20, n_informative
# ٢ . ﺿﺮاﯾﺐ ﻣﺴﯿﺮ ﻣﺤﺎﺳﺒﮫ
alphas_lasso, coefs_lasso, _ = lasso_path(X, y)
# ٣ . ﻧﻤﻮدار ﭘﻨﺠﺮه اﯾﺠﺎد
fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(11, 6))
log_alphas = np.log10(alphas_lasso)
# ۴ . ﻟﯿﺴﺖ ﯾﮏ در ھﺎ آن ذﺧﯿﺮه و ﺧﻄﻮط رﺳﻢ
lines = []
for i in range(coefs_lasso.shape[0]):
 initial_coef = coefs_lasso[i][-1]
# ﺧﻂ ھﺮ روی ﻟﯿﺒﻞ ذﺧﯿﺮه
line, = ax.plot(
 log_alphas, 
 coefs_lasso[i], 
 linewidth=2, 
 picker=True, 
 label=f'Feature {i+1} (Max w = {initial_coef:.1f})'
 )

# ٢ . ﺿﺮاﯾﺐ ﻣﺴﯿﺮ ﻣﺤﺎﺳﺒﮫ
alphas_lasso, coefs_lasso, _ = lasso_path(X, y)
# ٣ . (راھﻨﻤﺎ ﺟﺎﮔﯿﺮی ﺑﺮای ﺗﺮ ﻋﺮﯾﺾ ﮐﻤﯽ ) ﻧﻤﻮدار ﮐﺎدر ﺗﻨﻈﯿﻢ
plt.figure(figsize=(12, 6))
# ۴ . ﺑﺮای دﻗﯿﻖ ﻟﯿﺒﻞ ﺑﺎ ھﻤﺮاه ﺧﻂ ھﺮ رﺳﻢLegend
for i in range(coefs_lasso.shape[0]):
# (ﺿﺮﯾﺐ ﺑﺎﻻﺗﺮﯾﻦ = ﻻﻧﺪا ﮐﻤﺘﺮﯾﻦ ) ﻧﻤﻮدار راﺳﺖ ﺳﻤﺖ در ﺿﺮﯾﺐ اوﻟﯿﮫ ﻣﻘﺪار
initial_coef = coefs_lasso[i][-1]
# ﺿﺮﯾﺐ ﻣﻘﺪار و وﯾﮋﮔﯽ ﺷﻤﺎره ﺷﺎﻣﻞ ﻟﯿﺒﻞ
label_text = f'Feature {i+1} (w = {initial_coef:.1f})'

ﺗﺎ ﮐردم اﺿﺎﻓﮫ ﮐد اﻧﺗﮭﺎی در ﮐوﭼﮏ ﺧط ٢ ﻓﻘط و ﮐردم ﺣﻔظ را ﺧودت ﮐد ﺳﺎﺧﺗﺎر دﻗﯾﻘﺎً! ھﺳت ﺣواﺳم
:ﺷود ﻓﻌﺎل ﺧودت ﮐُد ھﻣﯾن روی( ﮐﻠﯾﮑﯽ ﺗوﻟﺗﯾپ ) mplcursors اﻣﮑﺎن ھﻣﺎن
import numpy as np
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
from sklearn.linear_model import lasso_path
from sklearn.datasets import make_regression
import mplcursors # اﯾﺠﺎد ﮐﺘﺎﺑﺨﺎﻧﮫTooltip ﺗﻌﺎﻣﻠﯽ ﮐﻠﯿﮏ و
 #١ .ﻧﻤﻮﻧﮫ داده ﺳﺎﺧﺖ
X, y = make_regression(n_samples=100, n_features=20, n_informative
# ٢ . ﺿﺮاﯾﺐ ﻣﺴﯿﺮ ﻣﺤﺎﺳﺒﮫ
alphas_lasso, coefs_lasso, _ = lasso_path(X, y)
# ٣ . (راھﻨﻤﺎ ﺟﺎﮔﯿﺮی ﺑﺮای ﺗﺮ ﻋﺮﯾﺾ ﮐﻤﯽ ) ﻧﻤﻮدار ﮐﺎدر ﺗﻨﻈﯿﻢ
plt.figure(figsize=(12, 6))
# ۴ . ﺑﺮای دﻗﯿﻖ ﻟﯿﺒﻞ ﺑﺎ ھﻤﺮاه ﺧﻂ ھﺮ رﺳﻢLegend
for i in range(coefs_lasso.shape[0]):
# (ﺿﺮﯾﺐ ﺑﺎﻻﺗﺮﯾﻦ = ﻻﻧﺪا ﮐﻤﺘﺮﯾﻦ ) ﻧﻤﻮدار راﺳﺖ ﺳﻤﺖ در ﺿﺮﯾﺐ اوﻟﯿﮫ ﻣﻘﺪار
initial_coef = coefs_lasso[i][-1]
# ﺿﺮﯾﺐ ﻣﻘﺪار و وﯾﮋﮔﯽ ﺷﻤﺎره ﺷﺎﻣﻞ ﻟﯿﺒﻞ
label_text = f'Feature {i+1} (w = {initial_coef:.1f})'

 plt.plot(np.log10(alphas_lasso), coefs_lasso[i], label=label_t
# ۵ . ﻋﻨﻮان و ﻣﺤﻮرھﺎ ﺗﻨﻈﯿﻤﺎت
plt.xlabel('log10(Lambda / Alpha)', fontsize=11)
plt.ylabel('Coefficients', fontsize=11)
plt.title('Lasso Path Plot with Coefficients Legend', fontsize=13
plt.grid(True)
plt.axhline(0, color='black', linestyle='--', linewidth=0.8)
plt.gca().invert_xaxis()

# ۶ . ﮐﺮدن اﺿﺎﻓﮫLegend راﺳﺖ ﮐﺎدر از ﺧﺎرج در ﻣﺮﺗﺐ و ﮐﺎﻣﻞ
================================================================ #
plt.legend(
 bbox_to_anchor=(1.02, 1), # راﺳﺖ ﺳﻤﺖ ﮐﺎدر از ﺑﯿﺮون ﺑﮫ اﻧﺘﻘﺎل
loc='upper left', # ﭼﭗ ﺳﻤﺖ ﺑﺎﻻ از ﭼﺴﺒﺎﻧﺪن
borderaxespad=0, # اﺿﺎﻓﯽ ﻓﺎﺻﻠﮫ ﺑﺪون
ncols=2, # ﻣﺠﺰا ﺳﺘﻮن ٢ در ﺳﺎزی ﻣﺮﺗﺐ
fontsize='small' # ﻣﺮﺗﺐ و ﺧﻮاﻧﺎ ﻓﻮﻧﺖ
)
 #اﻓﺘﺎدن از ﺟﻠﻮﮔﯿﺮی ﺟﮭﺖ ھﺎ ﺣﺎﺷﯿﮫ ﻓﺎﺻﻠﮫ ﺗﻨﻈﯿﻢLegend ﺻﻔﺤﮫ ﺑﯿﺮون ﺑﮫ
plt.tight_layout()
# ================================================================
# ﺷﺪه رﺳﻢ ﺧﻄﻮط ﺗﻤﺎم روی ﺗﻮﻟﺘﯿﭗ ﮐﺮدن اﺿﺎﻓﮫ: ﮐﻮﭼﮏ ﺗﻐﯿﯿﺮ
================================================================ #
cursor = mplcursors.cursor(hover=False) # ﮐﻠﯿﮏ ﺑﺎ ﺗﻮﻟﺘﯿﭗ اﯾﺠﺎد
cursor.connect("add", lambda sel: sel.annotation.set_text(
f"{sel.artist.get_label()}\nCoeff: {sel.target[1]:.2f}\nlog10
))
# ﭘﻮﯾﺎ ﭘﻨﺠﺮه ﻧﻤﺎﯾﺶ
plt.show()

import numpy as np
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
from sklearn.linear_model import lasso_path
from sklearn.datasets import make_regression
import mplcursors # اﯾﺠﺎد ﮐﺘﺎﺑﺨﺎﻧﮫTooltip ﺗﻌﺎﻣﻠﯽ ﮐﻠﯿﮏ و
 #١ .ﻧﻤﻮﻧﮫ داده ﺳﺎﺧﺖ
X, y = make_regression(n_samples=100, n_features=20, n_informative
# ٢ . ﺿﺮاﯾﺐ ﻣﺴﯿﺮ ﻣﺤﺎﺳﺒﮫ
alphas_lasso, coefs_lasso, _ = lasso_path(X, y)
# ٣ . ﻧﻤﻮدار ﮐﺎدر ﺗﻨﻈﯿﻢ
plt.figure(figsize=(12, 6))
# ۴ . ﺑﺮای دﻗﯿﻖ ﻟﯿﺒﻞ ﺑﺎ ھﻤﺮاه ﺧﻂ ھﺮ رﺳﻢLegend
for i in range(coefs_lasso.shape[0]):
 initial_coef = coefs_lasso[i][-1]
 label_text = f'Feature {i+1} (w = {initial_coef:.1f})'
 plt.plot(np.log10(alphas_lasso), coefs_lasso[i], label=label_t
# ۵ . ﻋﻨﻮان و ﻣﺤﻮرھﺎ ﺗﻨﻈﯿﻤﺎت

plt.ylabel('Coefficients', fontsize=11)
plt.title('Lasso Path Plot with Coefficients Legend', fontsize=13
plt.grid(True)

from sklearn.datasets import make_regression
import mplcursors # اﯾﺠﺎد ﮐﺘﺎﺑﺨﺎﻧﮫTooltip ﺗﻌﺎﻣﻠﯽ ﮐﻠﯿﮏ و

 label_text = f'Feature {i+1} (w = {initial_coef:.1f})'
 plt.plot(np.log10(alphas_lasso), coefs_lasso[i], label=label_t
# ۵ . ﻋﻨﻮان و ﻣﺤﻮرھﺎ ﺗﻨﻈﯿﻤﺎت
plt.xlabel('log10(Lambda / Alpha)', fontsize=11)
plt.ylabel('Coefficients', fontsize=11)
plt.title('Lasso Path Plot with Coefficients Legend', fontsize=13
plt.grid(True)

import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
from sklearn.datasets import make_regression
from sklearn.tree import DecisionTreeRegressor, plot_tree
# ١ . ﻧﻤﻮﻧﮫ داده ﺳﺎﺧﺖ
X, y = make_regression(n_samples=50, n_features=2, noise=10, rando
# ٢ . (ﺗﺮ ﺳﺎده ﻓﮭﻢ ﺑﺮای ٣ ﺑﮫ ﻋﻤﻖ ﮐﺮدن ﻣﺤﺪود ) ﺗﺼﻤﯿﻢ درﺧﺖ ﻣﺪل آﻣﻮزش و ﺳﺎﺧﺖ
model = DecisionTreeRegressor(max_depth=3, random_state=42)
model.fit(X, y)
# ٣ . ﺗﺼﻤﯿﻢ درﺧﺖ ﺳﺎﺧﺘﺎر ﮐﻞ رﺳﻢ!

دﯾد؟ ﺧواھﯾد ﭼﯾزی ﭼﮫ ﻋﮑس اﯾن ﺧروﺟﯽ در 
ﺷده ﮐﺷﯾده درﺧﺗﯽ ﻧﻣودار ﯾﮏ ﮐﻧﯾد، ﺑﺎز را decision_tree_structure.png ﻓﺎﯾل وﻗﺗﯽ
:اﺳت زﯾر ﻣوارد ﺷﺎﻣل ﮐﮫ ﺑﯾﻧﯾد ﻣﯽ
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
from sklearn.datasets import make_regression
from sklearn.tree import DecisionTreeRegressor, plot_tree
# ١ . ﺳﺎدﮔﯽ ﺑﺮای وﯾﮋﮔﯽ ٢ ﺑﺎ ﻧﻤﻮﻧﮫ داده ﺳﺎﺧﺖ
X, y = make_regression(n_samples=50, n_features=2, noise=10, rando
# ٢ . ﺗﺼﻤﯿﻢ درﺧﺖ ﻣﺪل آﻣﻮزش و ﺳﺎﺧﺖ
 #ﻧﮑﺘﮫ max_depth=3 ﻧﺸﻮد ﭘﯿﭽﯿﺪه ﺣﺪ از ﺑﯿﺶ ﺗﺎ ﮐﻨﺪ ﻣﯽ ﮐﻨﺘﺮل را درﺧﺖ ﻋﻤﻖ
model = DecisionTreeRegressor(max_depth=3, random_state=42)
model.fit(X, y)
# ٣ . درﺧﺖ ﺗﺼﻮﯾﺮ رﺳﻢ و اﻧﺪازه ﺗﻨﻈﯿﻢ
plt.figure(figsize=(14, 8))
plot_tree(
 model, 
 feature_names=['Feature 1', 'Feature 2'], 
 filled=True, # ﺑﯿﻨﯽ ﭘﯿﺶ ﻣﻘﺪار اﺳﺎس ﺑﺮ ھﺎ ﮔﺮه ﮐﺮدن رﻧﮕﯽ
rounded=True, # ﮐﺎدرھﺎ ھﺎی ﮔﻮﺷﮫ ﮐﺮدن ﮔﺮد
fontsize=9
)
plt.title("Decision Tree Structure (Max Depth = 3)", fontsize=14, 
# ۴ . ﭘﺮوژه ﻓﻮﻟﺪر در ﺧﺮوﺟﯽ ﻋﮑﺲ ذﺧﯿﺮه
output_file = 'decision_tree_structure.png'
plt.savefig(output_file, dpi=300, bbox_inches='tight')
print(f" ✅ ﻓﺎﯾﻞ در آن ﺗﺼﻮﯾﺮ! ﺷﺪ رﺳﻢ ﻣﻮﻓﻘﯿﺖ ﺑﺎ ﺗﺼﻤﯿﻢ درﺧﺖ' {output_file}' ﮔﺮدﯾﺪ 
ﺑﮫ ﺷرط اﯾن اﺳﺎس ﺑر را ھﺎ داده ﮔوﯾد ﻣﯽ ) ﻣﺛﻼً (:ﮐﺎدر ﺳﻘف ) ﺷﮑﺳﺗن ﺷرط

import matplotlib
matplotlib.use('Agg') # ﺧﻄﺎی از ﺟﻠﻮﮔﯿﺮیTcl درVS Code
import numpy as np
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
from sklearn.datasets import make_regression
from sklearn.tree import DecisionTreeRegressor
# ١ . (ﮐﻨﯿﻢ رﺳﻢ ﺑﻌﺪی ٢ ﺻﻔﺤﮫ روی ﺑﺘﻮاﻧﯿﻢ اﯾﻨﮑﮫ ﺑﺮای ) وﯾﮋﮔﯽ ٢ ﺑﺎ ﻧﻤﻮﻧﮫ داده ﺳﺎﺧﺖ
X, y = make_regression(n_samples=100, n_features=2, noise=15, rand
# ٢ . ﺗﺼﻤﯿﻢ درﺧﺖ ﻣﺪل آﻣﻮزش و ﺳﺎﺧﺖ

دﯾد؟ ﺧواھﯽ ﭼﯾزی ﭼﮫ ﺗﺻوﯾر اﯾن در 
model = DecisionTreeRegressor(max_depth=3, random_state=42)
model.fit(X, y)
# ٣ . ای ﺷﺒﮑﮫ ﺷﺒﮑﮫ اﯾﺠﺎدMeshgrid) ﻧﻤﻮدار ﺻﻔﺤﮫ ﺗﻤﺎم ﭘﻮﺷﺶ ﺑﺮای
x1_min, x1_max = X[:, 0].min() - 0.5, X[:, 0].max() + 0.5
x2_min, x2_max = X[:, 1].min() - 0.5, X[:, 1].max() + 0.5
xx1, xx2 = np.meshgrid(
 np.linspace(x1_min, x1_max, 200),
 np.linspace(x2_min, x2_max, 200)
)
# ۴ . ﻣﻘﺪار ﺑﯿﻨﯽ ﭘﯿﺶy ای ﺷﺒﮑﮫ ﺻﻔﺤﮫ اﯾﻦ ﻧﻘﺎط ﺗﻤﺎم ﺑﺮای
Z = model.predict(np.c_[xx1.ravel(), xx2.ravel()])
Z = Z.reshape(xx1.shape)
# ۵ . ھﺎ داده و ﺗﺼﻤﯿﻢ ﻣﺮزھﺎی ﻧﻤﻮدار رﺳﻢ
plt.figure(figsize=(10, 7))
# (درﺧﺘﯽ ﺗﺼﻤﯿﻢ ﻣﺮزھﺎی ) رﻧﮕﯽ ﻣﻨﺎطﻖ رﺳﻢ
contour = plt.contourf(xx1, xx2, Z, alpha=0.7, cmap='viridis')
plt.colorbar(contour, label='Predicted Value (y)')
# ھﺎ داده واﻗﻌﯽ ﻧﻘﺎط رﺳﻢ
scatter = plt.scatter(X[:, 0], X[:, 1], c=y, cmap='viridis', edgec
# ظﺎھﺮی ﺗﻨﻈﯿﻤﺎت
plt.xlabel('Feature 1', fontsize=11)
plt.ylabel('Feature 2', fontsize=11)
plt.title('Decision Tree Boundaries (Max Depth = 3)', fontsize=13
plt.grid(True, linestyle='--', alpha=0.3)
# ﺧﺮوﺟﯽ ﻋﮑﺲ ذﺧﯿﺮه
output_name = 'decision_tree_boundaries.png'
plt.savefig(output_name, dpi=300, bbox_inches='tight')
print(f" ✅ ﻓﺎﯾﻞ در را آن ﻋﮑﺲ! ﺷﺪ رﺳﻢ ﻣﻮﻓﻘﯿﺖ ﺑﺎ ﺗﺼﻤﯿﻢ ﻣﺮزھﺎی' {output_name}' ﯿﺪ

from sklearn.tree import DecisionTreeRegressor
# ١ . ﻧﻮﯾﺰ اﻧﺪﮐﯽ ﺑﺎ ﻧﻤﻮﻧﮫ داده ﺳﺎﺧﺖ

Z_pruned = tree_pruned.predict(np.c_[xx1.ravel(), xx2.ravel()]).re
# ۵ . ﻣﻘﺎﯾﺴﮫ ﺟﮭﺖ ھﻢ ﮐﻨﺎر ﻧﻤﻮدار دو رﺳﻢ

import matplotlib
matplotlib.use('Agg') # ﺧﻄﺎی از ﺟﻠﻮﮔﯿﺮیTcl
import numpy as np
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
from sklearn.datasets import make_regression
from sklearn.tree import DecisionTreeRegressor
from sklearn.ensemble import RandomForestRegressor
# ١ . ﻧﻮﯾﺰ ﺑﺎ ﻧﻤﻮﻧﮫ داده ﺳﺎﺧﺖ
X, y = make_regression(n_samples=100, n_features=2, noise=20, rand
# ٢ . ﻣﻨﻔﺮد ﺗﺼﻤﯿﻢ درﺧﺖ ﯾﮏ آﻣﻮزشvs ﺗﺼﺎدﻓﯽ ﺟﻨﮕﻞ ﯾﮏ
single_tree = DecisionTreeRegressor(max_depth=5, random_state=42)
random_forest = RandomForestRegressor(n_estimators=100, max_depth=
single_tree.fit(X, y)
random_forest.fit(X, y)
# ٣ . ﻣﺮزھﺎ رﺳﻢ ﺑﺮای ای ﺷﺒﮑﮫ ﺷﺒﮑﮫ
x1_min, x1_max = X[:, 0].min() - 0.5, X[:, 0].max() + 0.5
x2_min, x2_max = X[:, 1].min() - 0.5, X[:, 1].max() + 0.5

import matplotlib
matplotlib.use('Agg') # ﺧﻄﺎی از ﺟﻠﻮﮔﯿﺮیTcl
import numpy as np
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
from sklearn.datasets import make_regression
from sklearn.tree import DecisionTreeRegressor
from sklearn.ensemble import RandomForestRegressor
# ١ . ﻧﻮﯾﺰ ﺑﺎ ﻧﻤﻮﻧﮫ داده ﺳﺎﺧﺖ
X, y = make_regression(n_samples=100, n_features=2, noise=20, rand
# ٢ . ﻣﻨﻔﺮد ﺗﺼﻤﯿﻢ درﺧﺖ ﯾﮏ آﻣﻮزشvs ﺗﺼﺎدﻓﯽ ﺟﻨﮕﻞ ﯾﮏ
single_tree = DecisionTreeRegressor(max_depth=5, random_state=42)
random_forest = RandomForestRegressor(n_estimators=100, max_depth=
single_tree.fit(X, y)
random_forest.fit(X, y)
# ٣ . ﻣﺮزھﺎ رﺳﻢ ﺑﺮای ای ﺷﺒﮑﮫ ﺷﺒﮑﮫ
x1_min, x1_max = X[:, 0].min() - 0.5, X[:, 0].max() + 0.5
x2_min, x2_max = X[:, 1].min() - 0.5, X[:, 1].max() + 0.5

!اﺳت ﻣﺧﺗﻠف ﻧظر ٠٠١ ﮔﯾریِ ﻣﯾﺎﻧﮕﯾن ﺣﺎﺻلِ ﭼون اﻧد؛ ﺷده
xx1, xx2 = np.meshgrid(np.linspace(x1_min, x1_max, 200), np.linspa
Z_tree = single_tree.predict(np.c_[xx1.ravel(), xx2.ravel()]).res
Z_forest = random_forest.predict(np.c_[xx1.ravel(), xx2.ravel()])
# ۴ . ﻣﻘﺎﯾﺴﮫ و رﺳﻢ
fig, axes = plt.subplots(1, 2, figsize=(16, 6))
# درﺧﺖ ﺗﮏ: ١ ﻧﻤﻮدار
axes[0].contourf(xx1, xx2, Z_tree, alpha=0.7, cmap='viridis')
axes[0].scatter(X[:, 0], X[:, 1], c=y, cmap='viridis', edgecolor=
axes[0].set_title('Single Decision Tree (Sharp Boundaries)', fonts
axes[0].set_xlabel('Feature 1')
axes[0].set_ylabel('Feature 2')
# ﺗﺼﺎدﻓﯽ ﺟﻨﮕﻞ: ٢ ﻧﻤﻮدار
axes[1].contourf(xx1, xx2, Z_forest, alpha=0.7, cmap='viridis')
axes[1].scatter(X[:, 0], X[:, 1], c=y, cmap='viridis', edgecolor=
axes[1].set_title('Random Forest - 100 Trees (Smooth Boundaries)'
axes[1].set_xlabel('Feature 1')
plt.tight_layout()
output_file = 'random_forest_vs_single_tree.png'
plt.savefig(output_file, dpi=300, bbox_inches='tight')
print(f" ✅ ﺗﺼﻮﯾﺮ! ﺷﺪ ذﺧﯿﺮه ﺧﺮوﺟﯽ' {output_file}' ﮐﻨﯿﺪ ﺑﺮرﺳﯽ را". )

import time
import numpy as np
from sklearn.datasets import make_regression
from sklearn.model_selection import train_test_split
from sklearn.metrics import mean_squared_error
from sklearn.ensemble import GradientBoostingRegressor
from xgboost import XGBRegressor
from lightgbm import LGBMRegressor
# ١ . ﺳﺮﻋﺖ ﺗﻔﺎوت دﯾﺪن ﺑﺮای ﺑﺰرگ ﻧﻤﻮﻧﮫ داده ﯾﮏ ﺳﺎﺧﺖ
X, y = make_regression(n_samples=50000, n_features=30, noise=15, r
X_train, X_test, y_train, y_test = train_test_split(X, y, test_si
models = {
"Standard GBR": GradientBoostingRegressor(n_estimators=100, ra
"XGBoost": XGBRegressor(n_estimators=100, random_state=42, ver
"LightGBM": LGBMRegressor(n_estimators=100, random_state=42, v
}
print(f"{'Model':<15} | {'Time (sec)':<12} | {'Test MSE':<10}")
print("-" * 42)
for name, model in models.items():
 start_time = time.time()
 model.fit(X_train, y_train)
 elapsed_time = time.time() - start_time
 preds = model.predict(X_test)
 mse = mean_squared_error(y_test, preds)
 print(f"{name:<15} | {elapsed_time:<12.4f} | {mse:<10.2f}")

import time
import numpy as np
from sklearn.datasets import make_regression
from sklearn.model_selection import train_test_split
from sklearn.metrics import mean_squared_error
from sklearn.ensemble import GradientBoostingRegressor
from xgboost import XGBRegressor
from lightgbm import LGBMRegressor
# ١ . ﺳﺮﻋﺖ ﺗﻔﺎوت دﯾﺪن ﺑﺮای ﺑﺰرگ ﻧﻤﻮﻧﮫ داده ﯾﮏ ﺳﺎﺧﺖ
X, y = make_regression(n_samples=50000, n_features=30, noise=15, r
X_train, X_test, y_train, y_test = train_test_split(X, y, test_si
models = {
"Standard GBR": GradientBoostingRegressor(n_estimators=100, ra
"XGBoost": XGBRegressor(n_estimators=100, random_state=42, ver
"LightGBM": LGBMRegressor(n_estimators=100, random_state=42, v
}
print(f"{'Model':<15} | {'Time (sec)':<12} | {'Test MSE':<10}")
print("-" * 42)
for name, model in models.items():
 start_time = time.time()
 model.fit(X_train, y_train)
 elapsed_time = time.time() - start_time
 preds = model.predict(X_test)
 mse = mean_squared_error(y_test, preds)
 print(f"{name:<15} | {elapsed_time:<12.4f} | {mse:<10.2f}")

import matplotlib
matplotlib.use('Agg') # ﮔﺮاﻓﯿﮑﯽ ﺧﻄﺎی از ﺟﻠﻮﮔﯿﺮیTcl درVS Code
import numpy as np
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
from sklearn.datasets import make_regression
from xgboost import XGBRegressor
from lightgbm import LGBMRegressor
# ١ . ﻧﻤﻮﻧﮫ داده ﺳﺎﺧﺖ
X, y = make_regression(n_samples=200, n_features=2, noise=25, rand
# ٢ . ﻣﺪل دو آﻣﻮزش و ﺳﺎﺧﺖ
XGBoost (ﺳﻄﺢ ﺑﮫ ﺳﻄﺢ رﺷﺪLevel-wise)
xgb_model = XGBRegressor(n_estimators=50, max_depth=3, learning_ra
xgb_model.fit(X, y)
# LightGBM (ﺑﺮگ ﺑﮫ ﺑﺮگ رﺷﺪLeaf-wise)
lgb_model = LGBMRegressor(n_estimators=50, max_depth=3, num_leaves
lgb_model.fit(X, y)
# ٣ . ﺗﺼﻤﯿﻢ ﻣﺮزھﺎی رﺳﻢ ﺑﺮای ﺷﺒﮑﮫ ﺳﺎزی آﻣﺎده
x1_min, x1_max = X[:, 0].min() - 0.5, X[:, 0].max() + 0.5
x2_min, x2_max = X[:, 1].min() - 0.5, X[:, 1].max() + 0.5
xx1, xx2 = np.meshgrid(np.linspace(x1_min, x1_max, 250), np.linspa
Z_xgb = xgb_model.predict(np.c_[xx1.ravel(), xx2.ravel()]).reshape
Z_lgb = lgb_model.predict(np.c_[xx1.ravel(), xx2.ravel()]).reshape
# ۴ . ھﻢ ﮐﻨﺎر ﻧﻤﻮدار دو در ﻣﻘﺎﯾﺴﮫ و رﺳﻢ
fig, axes = plt.subplots(1, 2, figsize=(16, 6))
# ١ ﻧﻤﻮدارXGBoost
c1 = axes[0].contourf(xx1, xx2, Z_xgb, alpha=0.75, cmap='viridis'
axes[0].scatter(X[:, 0], X[:, 1], c=y, cmap='viridis', edgecolor=
axes[0].set_title('XGBoost (Level-wise Growth)', fontsize=13, font
axes[0].set_xlabel('Feature 1')
axes[0].set_ylabel('Feature 2')
fig.colorbar(c1, ax=axes[0], label='Predicted Value (y)')

import matplotlib
matplotlib.use('Agg') # ﺧﻄﺎی از ﺟﻠﻮﮔﯿﺮیTcl درVS Code
import numpy as np
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
from sklearn.datasets import make_regression
from sklearn.linear_model import Lasso
from sklearn.ensemble import RandomForestRegressor
from xgboost import XGBRegressor
from lightgbm import LGBMRegressor
# ﮔﯿﺮی ﺗﺼﻤﯿﻢ ﻣﺮزھﺎی رﺳﻢ ﺑﺮای ﮐﻤﮑﯽ ﺗﺎﺑﻊ
def plot_decision_boundary(ax, model, X, y, title, color_map='vir
 x1_min, x1_max = X[:, 0].min() - 0.5, X[:, 0].max() + 0.5
 x2_min, x2_max = X[:, 1].min() - 0.5, X[:, 1].max() + 0.5
 xx1, xx2 = np.meshgrid(np.linspace(x1_min, x1_max, 200), np.l
 Z = model.predict(np.c_[xx1.ravel(), xx2.ravel()]).reshape(xx1
 contour = ax.contourf(xx1, xx2, Z, alpha=0.75, cmap=color_map
 ax.scatter(X[:, 0], X[:, 1], c=y, cmap=color_map, edgecolor='
 ax.set_title(title, fontsize=11, fontweight='bold')
 ax.set_xlabel('Feature 1')
 ax.set_ylabel('Feature 2')
return contour
# ﺗﺎﯾﯽ ۴ ﮐﻠﯽ ﺷﮑﻞ ﺳﺎﺧﺖ
fig, axes = plt.subplots(2, 2, figsize=(15, 12))

import matplotlib
matplotlib.use('Agg') # ﺧﻄﺎی از ﺟﻠﻮﮔﯿﺮیTcl درVS Code
import numpy as np
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
from sklearn.datasets import make_regression
from sklearn.linear_model import Lasso
from sklearn.ensemble import RandomForestRegressor
from xgboost import XGBRegressor
from lightgbm import LGBMRegressor
# ﮔﯿﺮی ﺗﺼﻤﯿﻢ ﻣﺮزھﺎی رﺳﻢ ﺑﺮای ﮐﻤﮑﯽ ﺗﺎﺑﻊ
def plot_decision_boundary(ax, model, X, y, title, color_map='vir
 x1_min, x1_max = X[:, 0].min() - 0.5, X[:, 0].max() + 0.5
 x2_min, x2_max = X[:, 1].min() - 0.5, X[:, 1].max() + 0.5
 xx1, xx2 = np.meshgrid(np.linspace(x1_min, x1_max, 200), np.l
 Z = model.predict(np.c_[xx1.ravel(), xx2.ravel()]).reshape(xx1
 contour = ax.contourf(xx1, xx2, Z, alpha=0.75, cmap=color_map
 ax.scatter(X[:, 0], X[:, 1], c=y, cmap=color_map, edgecolor='
 ax.set_title(title, fontsize=11, fontweight='bold')
 ax.set_xlabel('Feature 1')
 ax.set_ylabel('Feature 2')
return contour
# ﺗﺎﯾﯽ ۴ ﮐﻠﯽ ﺷﮑﻞ ﺳﺎﺧﺖ
fig, axes = plt.subplots(2, 2, figsize=(15, 12))

)

 axes[0, 1], rf, X2, y2, 
"2. Small Data & No Tuning Needed -> Random Forest\n(Robust & 
)
# -------------------------------------------------------------
# ﻣﺘﻮﺳﻂ داده روی ﻣﻤﮑﻦ دﻗﺖ ﺑﯿﺸﺘﺮﯾﻦ: ٣ ﺳﻨﺎرﯾﻮXGBoost
# -------------------------------------------------------------
X3, y3 = make_regression(n_samples=300, n_features=2, noise=15, ra
xgb = XGBRegressor(n_estimators=100, max_depth=3, learning_rate=0
xgb.fit(X3, y3)
plot_decision_boundary(
 axes[1, 0], xgb, X3, y3, 
"3. Maximum Accuracy on Tabular Data -> XGBoost\n(Level-wise P
)
# -------------------------------------------------------------
# ﺑﺎﻻ ﺳﺮﻋﺖ ﺑﮫ ﻧﯿﺎز و ﺣﺠﯿﻢ ھﺎی داده: ۴ ﺳﻨﺎرﯾﻮLightGBM
# -------------------------------------------------------------
X4, y4 = make_regression(n_samples=1000, n_features=2, noise=10, r
lgbm = LGBMRegressor(n_estimators=100, num_leaves=15, learning_rat
lgbm.fit(X4, y4)
plot_decision_boundary(
 axes[1, 1], lgbm, X4, y4, 
"4. Big Data & High Speed -> LightGBM\n(Leaf-wise Fast Splitt
)

import matplotlib
matplotlib.use('Agg') # ﺧﻄﺎی از ﺟﻠﻮﮔﯿﺮیTcl درVS Code
import numpy as np
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
from sklearn.datasets import make_classification
from sklearn.linear_model import LogisticRegression
# ١ . (١ و ٠ ) دوﺗﺎﯾﯽ ﺑﻨﺪی طﺒﻘﮫ ﺑﺮای ﻧﻤﻮﻧﮫ ھﺎی داده ﺳﺎﺧﺖ
X, y = make_classification(
 n_samples=100, n_features=2, n_redundant=0, 
 n_clusters_per_class=1, random_state=42
)
# ٢ . ﻟﻮﺟﺴﺘﯿﮏ رﮔﺮﺳﯿﻮن ﻣﺪل آﻣﻮزش و ﺳﺎﺧﺖ
model = LogisticRegression()
model.fit(X, y)

import matplotlib
matplotlib.use('Agg') # ﺧﻄﺎی از ﺟﻠﻮﮔﯿﺮیTcl درVS Code
import numpy as np
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
from sklearn.datasets import make_classification
from sklearn.linear_model import LogisticRegression
# ١ . (١ و ٠ ) دوﺗﺎﯾﯽ ﺑﻨﺪی طﺒﻘﮫ ﺑﺮای ﻧﻤﻮﻧﮫ ھﺎی داده ﺳﺎﺧﺖ
X, y = make_classification(
 n_samples=100, n_features=2, n_redundant=0, 
 n_clusters_per_class=1, random_state=42
)
# ٢ . ﻟﻮﺟﺴﺘﯿﮏ رﮔﺮﺳﯿﻮن ﻣﺪل آﻣﻮزش و ﺳﺎﺧﺖ
model = LogisticRegression()
model.fit(X, y)

ﺑدھﯾم؟ اداﻣﮫ را ﻣوﺿوع ﮐدام اﺳت؛ ﺣﯾﺎﺗﯽ ﺑﺳﯾﺎر ﺑﻧدی طﺑﻘﮫ در ﺑﻌدی ﮔﺎم
 #٣ .اﺣﺘﻤﺎل ﻣﺮز رﺳﻢ ﺑﺮای ﺷﺒﮑﮫ ﺳﺎزی آﻣﺎده
x1_min, x1_max = X[:, 0].min() - 0.5, X[:, 0].max() + 0.5
x2_min, x2_max = X[:, 1].min() - 0.5, X[:, 1].max() + 0.5
xx1, xx2 = np.meshgrid(np.linspace(x1_min, x1_max, 200), np.linspa
# ﻧﻘﺎط ﺗﻤﺎم ﺑﺮای ١ ﮐﻼس اﺣﺘﻤﺎل ﺑﯿﻨﯽ ﭘﯿﺶ
Z = model.predict_proba(np.c_[xx1.ravel(), xx2.ravel()])[:, 1].res
# ۴ . اﺣﺘﻤﺎﻻت و ﺗﺼﻤﯿﻢ ﻣﺮز ﻧﻤﻮدار رﺳﻢ
plt.figure(figsize=(9, 6))
# (١ ﺗﺎ ٠ از ) اﺣﺘﻤﺎﻻت رﻧﮕﯽ طﯿﻒ رﺳﻢ
contour = plt.contourf(xx1, xx2, Z, alpha=0.8, cmap='coolwarm')
plt.colorbar(contour, label='Probability of Class 1')
# (اﺳﺖ ٠. ۵ دﻗﯿﻘﺎً اﺣﺘﻤﺎل ﮐﮫ ﺟﺎﯾﯽ ) ﺗﺼﻤﯿﻢ ﻣﺮز ﺧﻂ رﺳﻢ
plt.contour(xx1, xx2, Z, levels=[0.5], colors='black', linewidths=
# (١ و ٠ ﮐﻼس ) واﻗﻌﯽ ﻧﻘﺎط رﺳﻢ
scatter = plt.scatter(X[:, 0], X[:, 1], c=y, cmap='coolwarm', edge
plt.xlabel('Feature 1', fontsize=11)
plt.ylabel('Feature 2', fontsize=11)
plt.title('Logistic Regression Decision Boundary & Probabilities'
